  - economic growth/development
  - the worst economic crisis since the war
  - There will be no quick economic recovery.

### 447. [effort](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/effort) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈefət/` · **US:** `/ˈefərt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/eff/effor/effort__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/eff/effor/effort__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - The project was a team effort.
  - a joint/collaborative effort
  - a determined/conscious/special effort
  - I didn't really feel like going out, but I am glad I made the effort.

### 448. [decide](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/decide) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈsaɪd/` · **US:** `/dɪˈsaɪd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dec/decid/decide__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dec/decid/decide__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - It's up to you to decide.
  - You will have to decide soon.
  - I can't tell you what to do—you'll have to decide for yourself.
  - decide to do something We've decided not to go away after all.

### 449. [rate](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/rate_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/reɪt/` · **US:** `/reɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rat/rate_/rate__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rat/rate_/rate__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - rate (of something) Figures published today show another fall in the rate of inflation.
  - at a/the rate of something Most people walk at an average rate of five kilometres an hour.
  - at a… rate The number of reported crimes is increasing at an alarming rate.
  - rate (that)… At the rate you work, you'll never finish!

### 450. [strong](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/strong) *(adjective)*

- **Examples:**
  - He's strong enough to lift a car!
  - strong muscles
  - She wasn't a strong swimmer (= she could not swim well).

### 451. [possible](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/possible_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpɒsəbl/` · **US:** `/ˈpɑːsəbl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pos/possi/possible__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pos/possi/possible__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

  - it is possible for somebody to do something Would it be possible for me to leave a message for her?

- **UK:** `/ɑːm/` · **US:** `/ɑːrm/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/arm/arm__/arm__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/arm/arm__/arm__us_1.mp3)

- **UK:** `/truː/` · **US:** `/truː/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tru/true_/true__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tru/true_/true__us_1.mp3)

### 486. [break](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/break_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/breɪk/` · **US:** `/breɪk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bre/break/break__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bre/break/break__us_1.mp3)

### 487. [better](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/better_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbetə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈbetər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bet/bette/better__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bet/bette/better__us_4.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - We're hoping for better weather tomorrow.
  - Her work is getting better and better.
  - He is in a much better mood than usual.
  - The meal couldn't have been better.

### 488. [difference](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/difference) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdɪfrəns/` · **US:** `/ˈdɪfrəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dif/diffe/difference__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dif/diffe/difference__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Americans and Brits speak the same language, but there are big cultural differences.
  - difference between A and B There are no significant differences between the education systems of the two countries.
  - He was studying the similarities and differences between humans and animals.
  - I can never tell the difference (= distinguish) between the twins.

### 489. [thank](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/thank) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/θæŋk/` · **US:** `/θæŋk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/tha/thank/thank__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/tha/thank/thank__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - thank somebody for something I must write and thank Mary for the present.
  - In his speech, he thanked everyone for all their hard work.
  - thank somebody for doing something She said goodbye and thanked us for coming.
  - thank somebody There's no need to thank me—I enjoyed doing it.

### 490. [receive](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/receive) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈsiːv/` · **US:** `/rɪˈsiːv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rec/recei/receive__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rec/recei/receive__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - receive something
  - I was delighted to receive your letter.
  - She received many messages of support.
  - Police had received information about a planned post office robbery.

### 491. [value](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/value_1) *(noun)*

- **Examples:**
  - to go up/rise/increase in value
  - to go down/fall/decline in value

  - The value of the shares has continued to fall.

### 492. [international](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/international_1) *(adjective)*

  - an international organization/institution/agency

### 493. [building](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/building) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈbɪldɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˈbɪldɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bui/build/building__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bui/build/building__us_3.mp3)
- **Examples:**

### 494. [action](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/action_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈækʃn/` · **US:** `/ˈækʃn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/act/actio/action__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/act/actio/action__us_2.mp3)

  - Divers were close at hand, ready to go into action if the stunt went wrong.

### 495. [full](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/full_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/fʊl/` · **US:** `/fʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/ful/full_/full__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/ful/full_/full__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

### 496. [model](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/model_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈmɒdl/` · **US:** `/ˈmɑːdl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mod/model/model__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mod/model/model__us_2.mp3)

  - The architect had produced a scale model of the proposed shopping complex.

### 497. [join](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/join_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dʒɔɪn/` · **US:** `/dʒɔɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/j/joi/join_/join__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/j/joi/join_/join__us_1.mp3)

  - join A to/onto B Join one section of pipe to the next.

### 504. [agree](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/agree) *(verb)*

  - over the centuries Their techniques have evolved over the centuries.

### 586. [evidence](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/evidence_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈevɪdəns/` · **US:** `/ˈevɪdəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/evi/evide/evidence__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/evi/evide/evidence__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - evidence (of something) Researchers have found clear scientific evidence of a link between exposure to sun and skin cancer.

  - evidence that… There is not a shred of evidence that the meeting actually took place.

- **UK:** `/kɔːs/` · **US:** `/kɔːrs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cou/cours/course__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cou/cours/course__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - a French/chemistry course
  - course in something He took a course in Russian language and literature.
  - I want to do a course in art and design.
  - The department offers short courses in drama, fiction, and poetry.

### 598. [summer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/summer_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsʌmə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈsʌmər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sum/summe/summer__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sum/summe/summer__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - in the summer We're going away in the summer.
  - in the summer of 2019
  - this/next/last summer
  - two summers ago

### 599. [less](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/less_3) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/les/` · **US:** `/les/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/les/less_/less__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/les/less_/less__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - less expensive/likely/intelligent
  - less often/enthusiastically
  - less than… I read much less now than I used to.
  - The receptionist was less than (= not at all) helpful.

### 600. [realize](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/realize) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈrɪəlaɪz/` · **US:** `/ˈriːəlaɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rea/reali/realize__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rea/reali/realize__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - realize (that)… I didn’t realize (that) you were so unhappy.
  - The moment I saw her, I realized something was wrong.
  - I finally came to realize that he would never change.
  - It is important to realize that there are still potential problems.

## Words 601 – 700

### 601. [hundred](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hundred) *(number)*
- **UK:** `/ˈhʌndrəd/` · **US:** `/ˈhʌndrəd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hun/hundr/hundred__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hun/hundr/hundred__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - One hundred (of the children) have already been placed with foster families.

  - She must be over a hundred (= a hundred years old).

### 604. [likely](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/likely_1) *(adjective)*

- **Examples:**
  - I got all the answers wrong.
  - He was driving on the wrong side of the road.
  - Sorry, I must have dialled the wrong number.
  - You're holding the camera the wrong way up!

### 635. [involve](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/involve) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ɪnˈvɒlv/` · **US:** `/ɪnˈvɑːlv/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inv/invol/involve__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inv/invol/involve__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - involve something Any investment involves an element of risk.
  - involve doing something The process involves using steam to sterilize the instruments.
  - The test will involve answering questions about a photograph.
  - involve somebody/something doing something The job involves me travelling all over the country.

### 636. [defense](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/defence) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈfens/` · **US:** `/dɪˈfens/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/def/defen/defence__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/def/defen/defence__us_12.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - in defence of somebody/something soldiers who died in defence of their country
  - What points can be raised in defence of this argument?
  - in somebody's defence I have to say in her defence that she knew nothing about it beforehand.
  - When her brother was criticized she leapt to his defence.

### 637. [anyone](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/anyone) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈeniwʌn/` · **US:** `/ˈeniwʌn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/any/anyon/anyone__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/any/anyon/anyone__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Is anyone there?
  - Does anyone else want to come?
  - Did anyone see you?
  - Hardly anyone came.

### 638. [increase](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/increase_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɪŋkriːs/` · **US:** `/ˈɪŋkriːs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/inc/incre/increase__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/inc/incre/increase__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - price/tax/wage increases
  - Profits show a steady increase.
  - increase in something Last year the business saw a 3% increase in turnover.
  - a substantial increase in the number of products on the market

### 639. [security](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/security) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/sɪˈkjʊərəti/` · **US:** `/sɪˈkjʊrəti/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sec/secur/security__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sec/secur/security__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - national/homeland security (= the defence of a country)
  - airport security officers
  - security against something The bars are to provide security against break-ins.
  - They carried out security checks at the airport.

### 640. [bank](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bank_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bæŋk/` · **US:** `/bæŋk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/ban/bank_/bank__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/ban/bank_/bank__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - I don't have much money in the bank at the end of the month.
  - I need to go to the bank (= the local office of a bank).
  - We are now in competition with the big foreign banks.
  - Major banks are increasing the amount they lend to small companies.

### 641. [myself](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/myself) *(pronoun)*
- **UK:** `/maɪˈself/` · **US:** `/maɪˈself/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mys/mysel/myself__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mys/mysel/myself__us_3.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - I cut myself on a knife.
  - I wrote a message to myself.
  - I found myself unable to speak.
  - I haven't been feeling myself recently (= I have not felt well).

### 642. [certainly](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/certainly) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɜːtnli/` · **US:** `/ˈsɜːrtnli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/cer/certa/certainly__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/cer/certa/certainly__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Without treatment, she will almost certainly die.
  - Certainly, the early years are crucial to a child's development.
  - I'm certainly never going there again.
  - I certainly hope we can do this again.

### 643. [west](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/west_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/west/` · **US:** `/west/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/w/wes/west_/west__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/w/wes/west_/west__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - West Africa
  - the west coast of Scotland
  - on the west side of the building
  - Australia's west coast

### 644. [sport](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sport_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/spɔːt/` · **US:** `/spɔːrt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spo/sport/sport__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spo/sport/sport__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - There are excellent facilities for sport and recreation.
  - I'm not interested in sport.
  - It's very difficult to make a career in professional sport.
  - (British English) Do you do any sport?

### 645. [board](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/board_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bɔːd/` · **US:** `/bɔːrd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/boa/board/board__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/boa/board/board__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - He had ripped up the carpet, leaving only the bare boards.
  - She noticed a loose board in the fence.

### 646. [seek](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/seek) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/siːk/` · **US:** `/siːk/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/see/seek_/seek__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/see/seek_/seek__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - seek something/somebody Drivers are advised to seek alternative routes.
  - Police are not seeking anyone else in connection with the deaths.
  - seek for something/somebody (British English) They sought in vain for somewhere to shelter.

### 647. [per](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/per) *(preposition)*
- **UK:** `/pɜː(r)/` · **US:** `/pɜːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/per__/per__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/per__/per__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Rooms cost £50 per person, per night.
  - 60 miles per hour
  - France consumes around 200 000 tonnes of cocoa per year.
  - This country has a higher crime rate per 100 000 of the population than most other European countries.

### 648. [subject](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/subject_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsʌbdʒekt/` · **US:** `/ˈsʌbdʒekt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sub/subje/subject__gb_7.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sub/subje/subject__us_4_rr.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - At this stage these are proposals and are still subject to change.
  - The rent is subject to review every five years.
  - Flights are subject to delay because of the fog.

### 649. [officer](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/officer) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɒfɪsə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːfɪsər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/off/offic/officer__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/off/offic/officer__us_2_rr.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - army/military/naval officers
  - senior officers in the Royal Air Force
  - The matter was passed on to me, as your commanding officer.
  - He's a retired army intelligence officer.

### 650. [private](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/private_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈpraɪvət/` · **US:** `/ˈpraɪvət/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pri/priva/private__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pri/priva/private__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - The sign said, ‘Private property. Keep out.’
  - You're trespassing on private land.
  - a private jet
  - Those are my father's private papers.

### 651. [rest](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/rest_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rest/` · **US:** `/rest/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/rest_/rest__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/rest_/rest__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Take what you want and throw the rest away.
  - the rest of something The country enjoys friendly relations with the rest of the world.
  - How would you like to spend the rest of the day?
  - for the rest of something I'm not doing this job for the rest of my life.

### 652. [behavior](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/behaviour) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bɪˈheɪvjə(r)/` · **US:** `/bɪˈheɪvjər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/beh/behav/behaviour__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/beh/behav/behaviour__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - good/bad behaviour
  - social/sexual/criminal behaviour
  - a plan to tackle antisocial behaviour
  - behaviour towards somebody His behaviour towards her was becoming more and more aggressive.

### 653. [deal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/deal_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/diːl/` · **US:** `/diːl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dea/deal_/deal__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dea/deal_/deal__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - to sign/strike/finalize/close a deal
  - to negotiate/broker/agree/reach a deal
  - She is travelling to New York to seal the deal (= conclude it).
  - a deal with somebody We did a deal with the management on overtime.

### 654. [performance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/performance) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/pəˈfɔːməns/` · **US:** `/pərˈfɔːrməns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/per/perfo/performance__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/per/perfo/performance__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - The performance starts at seven.
  - an evening performance
  - They put on performances for the tourists.
  - This was one of the band's rare live performances.

### 655. [fight](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fight_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/faɪt/` · **US:** `/faɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fig/fight/fight__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fig/fight/fight__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - a street/gang fight
  - fight with somebody/something He got into a fight with a man in the bar.
  - (figurative) I had a fight with the ticket machine, which was being temperamental.
  - fight between A and B A fight broke out between rival groups of fans.

### 656. [throw](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/throw_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/θrəʊ/` · **US:** `/θrəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/thr/throw/throw__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/thr/throw/throw__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Practise throwing and catching.
  - They had a competition to see who could throw the furthest.
  - throw something Who threw that rock?
  - throw something + adv./prep. Stop throwing stones at the window!

### 657. [top](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/top_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/tɒp/` · **US:** `/tɑːp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/t/top/top__/top__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/t/top/top__/top__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - He lives on the top floor.
  - She kept her passport in the top drawer.
  - The score is in the top left-hand corner of the screen.

### 658. [quickly](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/quickly) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkwɪkli/` · **US:** `/ˈkwɪkli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/q/qui/quick/quickly__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/q/qui/quick/quickly__us_3.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - She walked quickly away.
  - The disease spreads quickly.
  - The last few weeks have gone quickly (= the time seems to have passed quickly).
  - The tool makes it possible to create websites quickly and easily.

### 659. [past](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/past_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/pɑːst/` · **US:** `/pæst/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pas/past_/past__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pas/past_/past__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - in past years/centuries
  - in times past
  - The time for discussion is past.
  - The days are long past when people worked at the same company all their lives.

### 660. [goal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/goal) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ɡəʊl/` · **US:** `/ɡəʊl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/g/goa/goal_/goal__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/g/goa/goal_/goal__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - He headed the ball into an open goal (= one that had nobody defending it).
  - in goal Nicky Roberts was outstanding in goal (= as goalkeeper).
  - The goalkeeper was injured so a defender had to go in goal.

### 661. [second](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/second1_2) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsekənd/` · **US:** `/ˈsekənd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sec/secon/second__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sec/secon/second__us_3.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - She came second in the marathon.
  - One of the smaller parties came a close second (= nearly won).
  - The women's team came in fourth and the men finished second.
  - I agreed to speak second.

### 662. [bed](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/bed_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/bed/` · **US:** `/bed/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/bed/bed__/bed__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/bed/bed__/bed__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - a single/double bed
  - on the bed She lay on the bed (= on top of the covers).
  - in bed He lay in bed (= under the covers).
  - I'm tired—I'm going to bed.

### 663. [order](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/order_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːdə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːrdər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/o/ord/order/order__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/o/ord/order/order__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - in… order The names are listed in alphabetical order.
  - The winning scores, in reverse order, are as follows.
  - in the/a… order Put the words in the correct order.
  - Let's take the problems in a different order.

### 664. [author](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/author_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɔːθə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈɔːθər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/aut/autho/author__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/aut/autho/author__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Who is your favourite author?
  - He is the author of three books on art.
  - best-selling author Paul Theroux
  - the lead author of the report

### 665. [fill](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fill_1) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/fɪl/` · **US:** `/fɪl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fil/fill_/fill__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fil/fill_/fill__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - fill something Please fill this glass for me.
  - to fill a space/vacuum/void
  - Smoke filled the room.
  - Her image filled the screen.

### 666. [represent](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/represent) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/ˌreprɪˈzent/` · **US:** `/ˌreprɪˈzent/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rep/repre/represent__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rep/repre/represent__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - The competition attracted over 500 contestants representing eight different countries.
  - Local businesses are well represented on the committee (= there are a lot of people from them on the committee).
  - The opening speech was by Bob Alan representing Amnesty International.
  - The President was represented at the ceremony by the Vice-President.

### 667. [focus](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/focus_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfəʊkəs/` · **US:** `/ˈfəʊkəs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/foc/focus/focus__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/foc/focus/focus__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - focus of something It was the main focus of attention at the meeting.
  - The primary focus of the book is practical teaching techniques.
  - He decided to make colour the central focus of his work.
  - focus for something His comments provided a focus for debate.

### 668. [foreign](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/foreign) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfɒrən/` · **US:** `/ˈfɔːrən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/for/forei/foreign__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/for/forei/foreign__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - What foreign languages do you speak?
  - It can be a challenge at first living in a foreign country.
  - I went to the bank to get some foreign currency.
  - a foreign-owned company

### 669. [drop](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/drop_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/drɒp/` · **US:** `/drɑːp/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dro/drop_/drop__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dro/drop_/drop__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - The first drops of rain fell.
  - There was a drop of blood on his finger.
  - a drop of water/oil/sweat
  - Mix a few drops of milk into the cake mixture.

### 670. [plan](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/plan_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/plæn/` · **US:** `/plæn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pla/plan_/plan__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pla/plan_/plan__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - plan for something Do you have any plans for the summer?
  - plan to do something There are no plans to build new offices.
  - Your best plan (= the best thing to do) would be to go by car.
  - There's been a change of plan.

### 671. [blood](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/blood_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/blʌd/` · **US:** `/blʌd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/b/blo/blood/blood__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/b/blo/blood/blood__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - He lost a lot of blood in the accident.
  - Blood was pouring out of a cut on her head.
  - His hands were covered in blood.
  - to give blood (= to have blood taken from you so that it can be used in the medical treatment of other people)

### 677. [no](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/no_2) *(determiner)*

### 679. [store](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/store_1) *(noun)*

- **Examples:**

  - to reduce costs/numbers
  - a reduced rate/price

### 681. [sound](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/sound_3) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/saʊnd/` · **US:** `/saʊnd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sou/sound/sound__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sou/sound/sound__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

  - This gives the design team a sound basis for their work.
  - The proposal makes sound commercial sense.

### 682. [note](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/note_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/nəʊt/` · **US:** `/nəʊt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/not/note_/note__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/not/note_/note__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

  - She made a mental note (= decided that she must remember) to ask Alan about it.
  - Note to self (= a mental instruction): book more holidays.

### 683. [fine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fine_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/faɪn/` · **US:** `/faɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fin/fine_/fine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fin/fine_/fine__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

### 684. [near](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/near_2) *(adjective)*

### 685. [movement](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/movement) *(noun)*

- **UK:** `/ˈentə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈentər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/e/ent/enter/enter__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/e/ent/enter/enter__us_4.mp3)

### 688. [share](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/share_3) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ʃeə(r)/` · **US:** `/ʃer/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sha/share/share__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sha/share/share__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

  - The party won its biggest share of the vote since 1992.
  - The new TV channel has continued to increase its share of advertising revenue.

### 689. [common](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/common_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈkɒmən/` · **US:** `/ˈkɑːmən/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/c/com/commo/common__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/c/com/commo/common__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - a common problem/occurrence/practice
  - a common feature/theme

### 690. [poor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/poor) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/pʊə(r)/` · **US:** `/pɔːr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/poo/poor_/poor__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/poo/poor_/poor__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Examples:**

  - There seem to be no other survivors.
  - I can't see you now—some other time, maybe.

### 692. [natural](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/natural_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈnætʃrəl/` · **US:** `/ˈnætʃrəl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/n/nat/natur/natural__gb_6.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/n/nat/natur/natural__us_1_rr.mp3)

  - wildlife in its natural habitat

- **UK:** `/reɪs/` · **US:** `/reɪs/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/rac/race_/race__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/rac/race_/race__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

  - Shall we have a race to the end of the beach?

### 694. [concern](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/concern_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/kənˈsɜːn/` · **US:** `/kənˈsɜːrn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconcern__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/x/xco/xconc/xconcern__us_3.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - concern about something/somebody Villagers expressed concern about the level of traffic.

  - concern over something/somebody The report raises serious concerns over safety at the plant.
  - concern for something/somebody She hasn't been seen for four days and there is concern for her safety.

### 695. [series](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/series) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɪəriːz/` · **US:** `/ˈsɪriːz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/ser/serie/series__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/ser/serie/series__us_2.mp3)

- **Examples:**

  - a significant amount/number/part/portion

### 697. [similar](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/similar) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ˈsɪmələ(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈsɪmələr/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sim/simil/similar__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sim/simil/similar__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

### 698. [hot](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/hot_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/hɒt/` · **US:** `/hɑːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/h/hot/hot__/hot__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/h/hot/hot__/hot__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**

### 699. [language](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/language) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈlæŋɡwɪdʒ/` · **US:** `/ˈlæŋɡwɪdʒ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lan/langu/language__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lan/langu/language__us_1.mp3)

### 700. [each](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/each) *(adverb)*

## Words 701 – 800

### 701. [usually](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/usually) *(adverb)*
- **UK:** `/ˈjuːʒəli/` · **US:** `/ˈjuːʒəli/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/u/usu/usual/usually__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/u/usu/usual/usually__us_1_rr.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - How long does the journey usually take?
  - Tension headache is not usually associated with any other symptoms.
  - For Americans, ‘mad’ usually means ‘angry’.
  - Usually, there's no extra charge for delivery.

### 702. [response](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/response) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/rɪˈspɒns/` · **US:** `/rɪˈspɑːns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/res/respo/response__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/res/respo/response__us_4.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - She made no response.
  - We have asked for a meeting and we are awaiting a response.
  - response to somebody/something I received an immediate response to my request.
  - Here's a quick response to your question.

### 703. [dead](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/dead_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/ded/` · **US:** `/ded/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dea/dead_/dead__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dea/dead_/dead__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - My mother's dead; she died in 2017.
  - a dead person/animal
  - dead leaves/wood/skin
  - Catherine's dead body lay peacefully on the bed.

### 704. [rise](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/rise_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/raɪz/` · **US:** `/raɪz/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/r/ris/rise_/rise__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/r/ris/rise_/rise__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - The industry is feeling the effects of recent price rises.
  - a tax rise
  - an interest rate rise
  - the rise and fall of daily temperatures

### 705. [animal](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/animal_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈænɪml/` · **US:** `/ˈænɪml/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/ani/anima/animal__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/ani/anima/animal__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - the animals and birds of South America
  - wild/farm animals
  - domestic animals such as dogs and cats
  - the export of live animals for slaughter

### 706. [factor](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/factor_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈfæktə(r)/` · **US:** `/ˈfæktər/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fac/facto/factor__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fac/facto/factor__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Obesity is a major risk factor for heart disease.
  - the key/crucial/deciding factor
  - Criminality is associated with a range of individual, family and environmental factors.
  - The result will depend on a number of different factors.

### 707. [decade](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/decade) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈkeɪd/` · **US:** `/dɪˈkeɪd/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dec/decad/decade__gb_3.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dec/decad/decade__us_4.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - the early decades of the nineteenth century
  - The nineties were a decade of rapid advances.
  - a career spanning five decades
  - over a decade He's changed a lot over the past decade.

### 708. [article](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/article) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈɑːtɪkl/` · **US:** `/ˈɑːrtɪkl/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/a/art/artic/article__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/a/art/artic/article__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - to read/write/publish an article
  - Her tweet follows an article posted online by the ‘Huffington Post’.
  - article (by somebody) on something I read an interesting article by a well-known scientist on the subject of genetics.
  - article (by somebody) about something Have you seen that article about young fashion designers?

### 709. [shoot](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/shoot_2) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ʃuːt/` · **US:** `/ʃuːt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sho/shoot/shoot__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sho/shoot/shoot__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - new green shoots

- **UK:** `/aɪˈdentɪfaɪ/` · **US:** `/aɪˈdentɪfaɪ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/i/ide/ident/identify__gb_2.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/i/ide/ident/identify__us_3.mp3)

  - identify somebody/something She was able to identify her attacker.
  - Passengers were asked to identify their own suitcases before they were put on the plane.
  - identify yourself Many of those arrested refused to identify themselves (= would not say who they were).
  - identify somebody/something as somebody/something The bodies were identified as those of two suspected drug dealers.

### 748. [left](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/left_1) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/left/` · **US:** `/left/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/l/lef/left_/left__gb_4.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/l/lef/left_/left__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Fewer people write with their left hand than with their right.
  - your left knee/leg/foot/arm
  - the left side of the field
  - The university is on the left bank of the river.

### 749. [meeting](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/meeting) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈmiːtɪŋ/` · **US:** `/ˈmiːtɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/m/mee/meeti/meeting__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/m/mee/meeti/meeting__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - A hundred people attended the public meeting.
  - The meeting will be held in the school hall.
  - to schedule/convene/arrange/organize/call a meeting
  - Helen will chair the meeting (= be in charge of it).

### 750. [determine](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/determine) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/dɪˈtɜːmɪn/` · **US:** `/dɪˈtɜːrmɪn/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/det/deter/determine__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/det/deter/determine__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - determine something An inquiry was set up to determine the cause of the accident.
  - Try to determine the extent of the problem.
  - determine what, whether, etc… We set out to determine exactly what happened that night.
  - It is difficult to determine when drama first appeared in India.

### 751. [prepare](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/prepare) *(verb)*
- **UK:** `/prɪˈpeə(r)/` · **US:** `/prɪˈper/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/p/pre/prepa/prepare__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/p/pre/prepa/prepare__us_4.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - prepare something/somebody to prepare a report
  - prepare something/somebody for somebody/something A hotel room is being prepared for them.
  - The college prepares students for a career in business.
  - prepare somebody/something to do something The training has prepared me to deal with any medical issue.

### 752. [disease](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/disease) *(noun)*

  - chronic diseases such as obesity and diabetes
  - health measures to prevent the spread of disease

### 754. [success](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/success) *(noun)*

### 758. [amount](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/amount_1) *(noun)*

### 759. [ability](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ability_1) *(noun)*

  - ‘Why weren't you at the meeting?’ ‘Sorry—I forgot.’

### 850. [since](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/since_3) *(adverb)*

  - He left home two weeks ago and we haven't heard from him since.

### 851. [claim](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/claim_2) *(noun)*

- **Examples:**
  - the mountains of Andalusia
  - snow-capped mountains
  - The steep mountains tower all around.

### 974. [distance](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/distance_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈdɪstəns/` · **US:** `/ˈdɪstəns/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/d/dis/dista/distance__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/d/dis/dista/distance__us_2.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - a short/long distance
  - Paul has to drive very long distances as part of his job.
  - a distance of something to travel/cover a distance of 200 kilometres
  - distance to something She walked the short distance to her apartment.

### 975. [spring](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/spring_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/sprɪŋ/` · **US:** `/sprɪŋ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/spr/sprin/spring__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/spr/sprin/spring__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - The following spring, the three artists travelled to California.
  - in (the) spring flowers that bloom in (the) spring
  - The birds arrive in late spring and leave again in early autumn.
  - He was born in the spring of 1944.

### 976. [fit](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/fit_2) *(adjective)*
- **UK:** `/fɪt/` · **US:** `/fɪt/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/f/fit/fit__/fit__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/f/fit/fit__/fit__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - Top athletes have to be very fit.
  - She tries to keep fit by jogging every day.
  - Hiking helps keep him physically fit.
  - All the recruits seemed fit and healthy.

### 977. [ring](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ring1_1) *(noun)*

### 978. [coast](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/coast_1) *(noun)*

  - a trip to the coast

  - on the coast a town on the south/east coast of England

### 979. [shadow](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/shadow_1) *(noun)*
- **UK:** `/ˈʃædəʊ/` · **US:** `/ˈʃædəʊ/` · [🔊 UK Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/uk_pron/s/sha/shado/shadow__gb_1.mp3) | [🔊 US Audio](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/media/english/us_pron/s/sha/shado/shadow__us_1.mp3)
- **Examples:**
  - The children were having fun, chasing each other's shadows.
  - As the curtains moved in the breeze, the shadows on the floor seemed to dance.
  - The ship's sail cast a shadow on the water.
  - The shadows lengthened as the sun went down.

### 986. [ocean](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/ocean) *(noun)*

  - The ship was dredged from the depths of the ocean.

### 989. [rock](https://www.oxfordlearnersdictionaries.com/definition/english/rock_1) *(noun)*
